## Semantic Chunks
### Section: Introduction
- **Level**: `intro`
- **Hash**: `5c46589a50fc272649bcb438c35d6e7d5c90abc8c39bdea5301797d631c2e289`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/should-teachers-run-both-ai-detection-and-plagiarism-detection` (Similarity: 0.9316)

```text
Yes. Schools must track Generative AI Writing, External Clipboard Copy/Pastes, and Traditional Plagiarism as three completely independent metrics—because each represents a fundamentally different pedagogical challenge that requires different evidence, instructional responses, and policy interventions.

Lumping all academic integrity issues into a single generic "similarity score" or "cheating flag" creates confusion and leads to misdiagnosed student problems. A student who forgets to cite a Wikipedia quote suffers from a citation mechanics issue ; a student who prompts ChatGPT to generate an essay suffers from an authorship outsourcing issue ; and a student who pastes an essay from Word suffers from a workflow tracking issue . By decoupling these three streams in Checkmark Plagiarism , educators gain the precision needed for fair evaluation and targeted instruction.

Below is an in-depth analysis of why multi-signal separation is essential for modern school governance.

Checkmark Plagiarism powers multi-signal separation by pairing AI detection with essay writing playback , plagiarism detection , autograding , and integrations with Canvas and Google Classroom .
```

### Section: The 3 Distinct Integrity Streams Explained
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `5b7889ec23f5435b80c866ff1e3293fecdc969dc0db609509e64aec0f79761df`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-can-ap-seminar-teachers-use-quote-anchored-rubric-feedback-to-accelerate-first-draft-research-scoring` (Similarity: 0.8577)

```text
1. Traditional Plagiarism

What it is: Uncredited matching of human-authored text from the web, journal articles, or peer submissions. Remedy: Citation and quotation instruction.

2. Generative AI Writing

What it is: Synthetic text generated by LLMs without a human original author. Remedy: AI policy education, oral defense, and prompt redesign.

3. External Paste Events

What it is: The physical behavioral act of inserting text from the clipboard into the document. Remedy: Keystroke timeline review and offline draft verification.
```

### Section: Why Separate Tracking Eliminates False Accusations
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `ba37ee35b09c238ef30aed30a706490bf9a4ed24a5db0ac1d310c84612172a29`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-can-teachers-detect-ai-use-without-copy-and-paste-evidence` (Similarity: 0.8642)

```text
Tracking these streams independently prevents serious grading errors:

The "Legitimate Quote" Case: A student who pastes a block quote from a primary source will show high paste volume and high plagiarism match, but 0% AI probability. Playback proves the student properly cited the passage. 
 The "Stealth AI" Case: An AI-generated essay that bypassed static detectors will show 0% plagiarism and 10% AI score, but 100% paste volume in 0.05 seconds, immediately exposing the shortcut. 
 The "Articulate Student" Case: A highly structured human essay might trigger a 70% AI score, but shows 0% plagiarism and 4 hours of typing with 0 paste events, conclusively proving authentic human composition.

Read more in how Checkmark writing process analysis works .
```

### Section: Comparison: Blended Single Score vs. Checkmark Independent Multi-Stream Telemetry
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `9946bc6e62a14fcdd266be8c870373d614652e06bddcf0938301ac93a22804c0`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/what-questions-should-a-school-ask-before-buying-an-ai-detector` (Similarity: 0.8762)

```text
Blended Single Score (Legacy Checkers)

Combines AI and plagiarism into one ambiguous percentage. 
 Teacher cannot tell if the issue is citations, AI, or peer sharing. 
 Blind to active drafting hours and paste timestamps. 
 Leads to misapplied penalties and student confusion.

Checkmark Independent Multi-Stream Telemetry

Independent scores for Plagiarism, AI, and Paste Volume. 
 Side-by-side Source Cards + Paragraph AI Heatmaps. 
 Accelerated video playback showing true typing hours. 
 Enables precise, customized educational interventions.
```

### Section: A 5-Step Educator Protocol for Multi-Stream Triage
- **Level**: `h2`
- **Hash**: `9aa154529819f28b97fa76bebb998b16dbb403c573c75c7fbf387484bf78f7e6`
- **Best Competitor Match**: `/learning/2026/8/how-can-i-find-the-original-source-a-student-copied-from` (Similarity: 0.8241)

```text
Multi-Stream Triage Protocol:

1. Open the Checkmark Report inside Canvas SpeedGrader or Google Classroom. 
 2. Check the Plagiarism Similarity score: determine if matched text is cited. 
 3. Check the AI Probability score: identify low-perplexity synthetic paragraphs. 
 4. Check the Paste Volume & Playback: verify active typing duration and paste logs. 
 5. Select the appropriate instructional remedy based on the specific stream flagged.
```
